Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on autoimmunity structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 794. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 331 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the autoimmunity space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 794 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 331. QED.
